Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hash operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 154. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 269 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on hash structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 154. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 269. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a hash algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 231. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 979/4.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 231 + O(1/n^979). Summing over all modes and applying the hash structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
